We show that leveraging more fully the expressivity of the braid representation and using it to condition the trajectories themselves leads to even further gains in joint prediction performance, with negligible added complexity either in training or at inference time. We do so by proposing a novel auxiliary task, braid prediction, done in parallel with the trajectory prediction task. By classifying edges between agents into their correct crossing types in the braid representation, the braid prediction task is able to imbue the model with improved social awareness, which is reflected in joint predictions that more closely adhere to the actual multi-agent behavior. This simple auxiliary task allowed us to obtain significant improvements in joint metrics on three separate datasets. We show how the braid prediction task infuses the model with future intention awareness, leading to more accurate joint predictions. Code is available at github.com/caiocj1/traj-pred-braid-theory.

</details>


### [336] [A Context Engineering Framework for Improving Enterprise AI Agents based on Digital-Twin MDP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22083)
*Xi Yang,Aurelie Lozano,Naoki Abe,Bhavya,Saurabh Jha,Noah Zheutlin,Rohan R. Arora,Yu Deng,Daby M. Sow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DT-MDP-CE框架，通过离线强化学习改进企业环境中的LLM智能体，解决数据质量、复杂推理和反馈信号不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业自动化AI智能体的实际部署和性能提升受到以下限制：数据质量和数量有限、复杂现实世界推理需求、自我对弈困难以及缺乏可靠反馈信号。

Method: 提出DT-MDP-CE框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 数字孪生马尔可夫决策过程(DT-MDP)，将智能体推理行为抽象为有限MDP；2) 鲁棒对比逆强化学习，基于DT-MDP高效估计奖励函数并从混合质量离线轨迹中推导策略；3) RL引导的上下文工程，使用前两步获得的策略改进智能体决策行为。

Result: 在IT自动化领域的代表性任务上进行案例研究，广泛的实验结果表明，相比基线智能体在各种评估设置下都取得了持续且显著的改进，表明该框架可推广到具有类似特征的其他企业环境智能体。

Conclusion: DT-MDP-CE框架能够有效解决企业AI智能体部署中的关键挑战，通过离线强化学习显著提升智能体性能，并具有模型无关性和轻量级特性，适用于多种企业环境。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in AI agents for enterprise automation and decision-making, their real-world deployment and further performance gains remain constrained by limited data quality and quantity, complex real-world reasoning demands, difficulties with self-play, and the lack of reliable feedback signals. To address these challenges, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic framework for improving LLM-based enterprise agents via offline reinforcement learning (RL). The proposed Context Engineering via DT-MDP (DT-MDP-CE) framework comprises three key components: (1) A Digital-Twin Markov Decision Process (DT-MDP), which abstracts the agent's reasoning behavior as a finite MDP; (2) A robust contrastive inverse RL, which, armed with the DT-MDP, to efficiently estimate a well-founded reward function and induces policies from mixed-quality offline trajectories; and (3) RL-guided context engineering, which uses the policy obtained from the integrated process of (1) and (2), to improve the agent's decision-making behavior. As a case study, we apply the framework to a representative task in the enterprise-oriented domain of IT automation. Extensive experimental results demonstrate consistent and significant improvements over baseline agents across a wide range of evaluation settings, suggesting that the framework can generalize to other agents sharing similar characteristics in enterprise environments.

</details>


### [337] [GSEM: Graph-based Self-Evolving Memory for Experience Augmented Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22096)
*Xiao Han,Yuzheng Fan,Sendong Zhao,Haochun Wang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GSEM：基于图的自演化记忆框架，通过双图层记忆图组织临床经验，支持适用性感知检索和在线反馈驱动的节点质量与边权重校准，在临床决策任务中超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法将经验存储为独立记录，缺乏显式关系结构，导致检索噪声、重用不可靠，有时甚至比直接LLM推理性能更差。需要一种能捕捉经验内部决策结构和经验间关系依赖的记忆框架。

Method: 提出GSEM框架，将临床经验组织成双图层记忆图：一层捕捉单个经验内的决策结构，另一层捕捉经验间的关系依赖。支持适用性感知检索和在线反馈驱动的节点质量与边权重校准。

Result: 在MedR-Bench和MedAgentsBench基准测试中，使用DeepSeek-V3.2和Qwen3.5-35B两个LLM骨干，GSEM在所有基线中达到最高平均准确率，分别为70.90%和69.24%。

Conclusion: GSEM通过图结构组织临床经验，有效解决了传统记忆增强方法的局限性，在临床决策任务中实现了更可靠的检索和重用，显著提升了决策准确性。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making agents can benefit from reusing prior decision experience. However, many memory-augmented methods store experiences as independent records without explicit relational structure, which may introduce noisy retrieval, unreliable reuse, and in some cases even hurt performance compared to direct LLM inference. We propose GSEM (Graph-based Self-Evolving Memory), a clinical memory framework that organizes clinical experiences into a dual-layer memory graph, capturing both the decision structure within each experience and the relational dependencies across experiences, and supporting applicability-aware retrieval and online feedback-driven calibration of node quality and edge weights. Across MedR-Bench and MedAgentsBench with two LLM backbones, GSEM achieves the highest average accuracy among all baselines, reaching 70.90\% and 69.24\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and Qwen3.5-35B, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/xhan1022/gsem.

</details>


### [338] [SpecTM: Spectral Targeted Masking for Trustworthy Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22097)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecTM（光谱目标掩码），一种物理信息掩码设计，通过预训练期间鼓励从跨光谱上下文重建目标波段，提升地球观测基础模型的物理约束和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地球观测基础模型通常依赖随机掩码，缺乏明确的物理约束，这在指导公共卫生决策的预测模型中是一个关键的可信度限制。需要开发能够编码光谱内在表示的物理信息学习方法。

Method: 提出SpecTM物理信息掩码设计，开发了可适应的多任务自监督学习框架，包括波段重建、生物光学指数推断和8天前时间预测，通过联合优化编码光谱内在表示。

Result: 在NASA PACE高光谱影像的微囊藻毒素浓度回归任务中，SpecTM达到R²=0.695（当前周）和R²=0.620（8天前预测），分别比基线模型提升+34%和+99%。目标掩码比随机掩码提升+0.037 R²，在极端稀缺条件下具有2.2倍的标签效率优势。

Conclusion: SpecTM实现了跨地球观测领域的物理信息表示学习，提高了基础模型的可解释性，为物理约束的自监督学习提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Foundation models are now increasingly being developed for Earth observation (EO), yet they often rely on stochastic masking that do not explicitly enforce physics constraints; a critical trustworthiness limitation, in particular for predictive models that guide public health decisions. In this work, we propose SpecTM (Spectral Targeted Masking), a physics-informed masking design that encourages the reconstruction of targeted bands from cross-spectral context during pretraining. To achieve this, we developed an adaptable multi-task (band reconstruction, bio-optical index inference, and 8-day-ahead temporal prediction) self-supervised learning (SSL) framework that encodes spectrally intrinsic representations via joint optimization, and evaluated it on a downstream microcystin concentration regression model using NASA PACE hyperspectral imagery over Lake Erie. SpecTM achieves R^2 = 0.695 (current week) and R^2 = 0.620 (8-day-ahead) predictions surpassing all baseline models by (+34% (0.51 Ridge) and +99% (SVR 0.31)) respectively. Our ablation experiments show targeted masking improves predictions by +0.037 R^2 over random masking. Furthermore, it outperforms strong baselines with 2.2x superior label efficiency under extreme scarcity. SpecTM enables physics-informed representation learning across EO domains and improves the interpretability of foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [339] [SURF: Signature-Retained Fast Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21002)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Sihui Ji,Yuan Gao,Liang Hou,Xin Tao,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: SURF：一种高效的高分辨率视频生成框架，通过两阶段方法（预览+精炼）在保持原始模型特征的同时实现12.5倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高分辨率视频生成面临严重的推理速度限制（如Wan2.1生成720p视频需50分钟以上），现有加速方法大多会损害原始模型的布局、语义、运动等特征

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）预览阶段：在最优分辨率下推理并下采样潜在空间生成低分辨率预览，采用无训练噪声重移位技术缓解分辨率降低带来的特征损失；2）精炼阶段：设计Refiner通过建立预览与高分辨率目标的映射关系来减少去噪步骤，集成移位窗口并精心设计训练范式

Result: SURF实现了显著加速：Wan 2.1生成5秒16fps 720p视频加速12.5倍，HunyuanVideo生成5秒24fps 720p视频加速8.7倍，同时最大程度保持原始模型特征

Conclusion: SURF提供了一种概念简单、兼容多种基础模型和加速方法的插件式框架，能够在高效生成高分辨率视频的同时，最大程度接近给定预训练模型的特征签名

Abstract: The demand for high-resolution video generation is growing rapidly. However, the generation resolution is severely constrained by slow inference speeds. For instance, Wan2.1 requires over 50 minutes to generate a single 720p video. While previous works explore accelerating video generation from various aspects, most of them compromise the distinctive signatures (e.g., layout, semantic, motion) of the original model. In this work, we propose SURF, an efficient framework for generating high-resolution videos, while maximally keeping the signatures. Specifically, SURF divides video generation into two stages: First, we leverage the pretrained model to infer at optimal resolution and downsample latent to generate low-resolution previews in fast speed; then we design a Refiner to upscale the preview. In the preview stage, we identify that directly inferring a model (trained with higher resolution) on lower resolution causes severe losses in signatures. So we introduce noise reshifting, a training-free technique that mitigates this issue by conducting initial denoising steps on the original resolution and switching to low resolution in later steps. In the refine stage, we establish a mapping relationship between the preview and the high-resolution target, which significantly reduces the denoising steps. We further integrate shifting windows and carefully design the training paradigm to get a powerful and efficient Refiner. In this way, SURF enables generating high-resolution videos efficiently while maximally closer to the signatures of the given pretrained model. SURF is conceptually simple and could serve as a plug-in that is compatible with various base model and acceleration methods. For example, it achieves 12.5x speedup for generating 5-second, 16fps, 720p Wan 2.1 videos and 8.7x speedup for generating 5-second, 24fps, 720p HunyuanVideo.

</details>


### [340] [MineRobot: A Unified Framework for Kinematics Modeling and Solving of Underground Mining Robots in Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22055)
*Shengzhe Hou,Xinming Lu,Tianyu Zhang,Changqing Yan,Xingli Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MineRobot是一个用于地下采矿机器人运动学建模与求解的统一框架，通过专用描述格式、拓扑处理管道和基于独立拓扑等效路径的求解器，实现实时运动学计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下采矿机器人通常采用闭环机构（如线性驱动器和平面四杆机构），其运动学建模和实时求解比典型的开链工业机械臂更具挑战性，需要避免危险的实际现场试验。

Method: 1. 提出采矿机器人描述格式（MRDF），为采矿机器人提供具有执行器和闭环语义的参数化运动学表示；2. 开发拓扑处理管道，将四杆子结构收缩为广义关节，并为每个执行器提取独立拓扑等效路径（ITEP）；3. 利用ITEP独立性，构建基于执行器的顺序正向运动学管道；4. 基于相同分解，将逆运动学公式化为边界约束优化问题，采用类高斯-赛德尔过程求解。

Result: 实验表明，MineRobot能够满足虚拟环境应用所需的实时性能和鲁棒性要求，通过将耦合的闭环运动学转换为一系列小型拓扑感知求解，避免了机器人特定的手动推导。

Conclusion: MineRobot为地下采矿机器人提供了一个统一的运动学建模和求解框架，支持高效计算，适用于虚拟环境中的训练、规划和数字孪生应用。

Abstract: Underground mining robots are increasingly operated in virtual environments (VEs) for training, planning, and digital-twin applications, where reliable kinematics is essential for avoiding hazardous in-situ trials. Unlike typical open-chain industrial manipulators, mining robots are often closed-chain mechanisms driven by linear actuators and involving planar four-bar linkages, which makes both kinematics modeling and real-time solving challenging. We present \emph{MineRobot}, a unified framework for modeling and solving the kinematics of underground mining robots in VEs. First, we introduce the Mining Robot Description Format (MRDF), a domain-specific representation that parameterizes kinematics for mining robots with native semantics for actuators and loop closures. Second, we develop a topology-processing pipeline that contracts four-bar substructures into generalized joints and, for each actuator, extracts an Independent Topologically Equivalent Path (ITEP), which is classified into one of four canonical types. Third, leveraging ITEP independence, we compose per-type solvers into an actuator-centered sequential forward-kinematics (FK) pipeline. Building on the same decomposition, we formulate inverse kinematics (IK) as a bound-constrained optimization problem and solve it with a Gauss--Seidel-style procedure that alternates actuator-length updates. By converting coupled closed-loop kinematics into a sequence of small topology-aware solves, the framework avoids robot-specific hand derivations and supports efficient computation. Experiments demonstrate that MineRobot provides the real-time performance and robustness required by VE applications.

</details>
